Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on power structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 583. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 573 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_583(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 583. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^573(S, F) is trivial. QED.
